Let's design a robust search:
1. Precompute fast convolution and score functions.
2. Generate initial sequences for several lengths: root d_i normalized, shifted variants, parent if available, random smooth, maybe low-rank perturbations.
3. Optimize each with:

LSE objective for z:
```
def lse_z_obj(x, beta, beta_changed? no,
    n=len(x); z=x - np.mean(x); z-=max; el=np.exp(z); p=el/el.sum()
    c=conv_square(p); cmax=max; scaled=beta*(c-cmax); e=np.exp(np.maximum(scaled,-745)); q=e/e.sum(); L=cmax + np.log(e.sum())/beta
    h = conv_q_p(q,p)
    # objective score = 2n*L (S=1)
    obj=2*n*L
    # gradient wrt z with respect to original x: as derived 4n p(h-L); and subtract mean of gradient? Let's verify with z transform.
    g = 4.0*n*p*(h-L)
    g -= g.mean()  # remove translation nullspace? We saw sum g=4n(Σp h -L)=0 if S=1. h sum property? h_i from q; Σ p_i h_i =Σ_k q_k c_k = L. So sum g=0. Due numerical, subtract mean.
    return obj,g
```
Check gradient for z transform: F(x)=f(z=x-mean). g_f = gradient wrt z. Original grad = g_f - mean(g_f) 1. Since sum g_f ≈0, g = g. Good.

But is objective f(z)=2nL with p softmax sum1. dF/dz_i = p_i * dF/dp_i. dF/dp_i =4n h_i? Wait objective F=2nL, where L LSE of c. S=1. Derivative dL/dp_i = 2h_i; no -L term because S fixed exactly by softmax. In z variables, p changes under z, but softmax ensures S=1. The derivative of L w.r.t z_i = Σ_j (dL/dp_j)(p_jδij - p_i p_j) = p_i dL/dp_i - p_i Σp_j dL/dp_j. Σp dL=2L. Thus gradient z =4n p_i h_i -4nL p_i =4n p_i(h_i-L). Yes correct. So our g is exact. Good.

LSE objective reduced:
```
def lse_reduced_obj(x, beta, n):
    z = np.empty(n); z[:-1]=x; z[-1]=-np.sum(x)
    z -= z.max()
    p = np.exp(z); p /= p.sum()
    c = conv_square(p, n)
    cmax = c.max()
    scaled = beta*(c-cmax)
    e = np.exp(np.maximum(scaled, -745.0))
    e_sum = e.sum()
    q = e/e_sum
    L = cmax + np.log(e_sum)/beta
    h = conv_q_p(q,p,n)
    obj = 2.0*n*L
    g = 4.0*n*p*(h - L)
    g_sum = g.sum()
    g -= g_sum/n  # ensure sum zero
    # but due parameterization, maybe true gradient for z has sum zero; after recenter mean, gradient in z space should be g (sum zero)
    gx = g[:-1] - g[-1]
    return obj, gx
```
But if we subtract mean from g, g still sum zero; okay. Wait if we recenter z by subtract max only (not mean? z built mean zero already, then subtract max, p unchanged; gradient with respect to x? Softmax stable can subtract max, but that is nonlinear constant shift; F invariant to shift, so constant max with x doesn't change F but gradient chain? If z0 = x_full - C(x), where C = max(x), F = f(z0) and f shift invariant, so F(x)=f(x_full). Actually subtracting max is just reparameterization by shift; since f invariant to adding constant, F same as f(x_full). Derivative of f(x_full) known; stable subtract doesn't change mathematically. LP update details:
```
def lp_iteration(a, M, A, time_limit):
    c = -np.ones(len(a))
    b_ub = np.full(A.shape[0], M)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * len(a)
    res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': time_limit, 'presolve': True})
    if res.x is not None: return np.asarray(res.x)
    return None
```

```
def solve_lp_for_update(p, n, time_limit=20.0):
    S=p.sum(); p=p/S
    c=conv_square_p(p,n,next_pow2(2n-1)); M=float(c.max())
    A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=float)
    for k in range(2*n-1): ...
    c_obj=-np.ones(n)
    b_ub=np.full(A.shape[0], M)
    bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n
    try: res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': int(time_limit), 'presolve': True})
    except Exception: return None
    if res.x is not None:
       b=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float); b=np.maximum(b,0); S=b.sum()
       if S>1e-8: return b/S
    return None
```

```
def adam_lse(z, n, m_c, m_h, deadline, max_steps, lr, beta_start, beta_end, label):
   z=z-z.mean(); m=zeros; v=zeros; best...; beta=beta_start
   for step in range(max_steps):
      if time > deadline: break
      p=softmax_z(z); c=conv_square; q etc; L; h; g=4n p(h-L); g-=g.mean()
      m=0.9m+0.1g; v=0.99v+0.01*g*g; z -= lr*m/(np.sqrt(v)+1e-8); z-=z.mean()
      # schedule beta increase every 500 steps to beta_end
      beta = min(beta_end, beta*1.05)
      if step%100==0: score exact; update best
   return best_p, best_sc
```

Function signature:
```
def polish_lbfgs(p, n, m_c, m_h, betas, maxfun_per, deadline, label, restart_x=None):
    p = np.maximum(p,0.0); S=p.sum(); if S<=... return p,inf
    p = p/S
    best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=score_p(p,n,m_c)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
    x=z[:-1].copy()
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       # If x has nonfinite reset
       try:
          res=minimize(lse_reduced_obj, x, args=(beta,n,m_c,m_h), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':maxfun_per,'maxiter':max(1,maxfun_per)})
          x_res = res.x if res.x is not None else x
          # reconstruct p
          z_full=...; p_new=softmax; sc=score_p
          if sc<best_sc: best...
          # set x for next to improved reduced maybe
          if sc<last_sc: x = p_to_reduced(p_new,n)
       except Exception: pass
    return best_p,best_sc
```

```
def peak_adam_z(p, n, m_c, deadline, max_steps=5000, kpeaks=5, lr=0.02, label=''):
   z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z-=z.mean(); m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); best...
   for st in range(max_steps):
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      p=softmax_z(z)
      c=conv_square_p(p,n,m_c)
      if kpeaks < len(c): peaks=np.argpartition(c, -kpeaks)[-kpeaks:]
      else: peaks=np.arange(len(c))
      L=float(np.mean(c[peaks]))
      h=np.zeros(n)
      kcnt=len(peaks)
      inv=1.0/kcnt
      for kk in peaks:
         k=int(kk); js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(k,n-1)
         if je>=js:
            h[js:je+1] += inv * p[k-je:k-js+1][::-1]
      g=4.0*n*p*(h-L)
      g-=g.mean()
      # if not finite continue
      m=0.9*m+0.1*g; v=0.99*v+0.01*g*g
      step=m/(np.sqrt(v)+1e-8)
      max_step=float(np.max(np.abs(step)))
      if max_step>1e-8:
         step=step*(lr/max_step if max_step>lr? Wait this normalizes max update to lr? We want update magnitude ~lr. Could do `z -= (lr / max_step) * step` if max_step>lr? That caps max update at lr. But Adam step magnitude around 1; lr=0.02, so yes `scale = lr / max_step` if max_step>lr else 1? To use adaptive, simpler `z -= lr * step`; step RMS ~1, max maybe few; okay. Cap max update: if max_step*lr > 0.2, scale=0.2/(max_step*lr). Use `z -= scale*lr*step`.
      z-=z.mean()
      if np.max(np.abs(z))>80: z*=80/max
      if st%100==0: sc=score_p(p,n,m_c); update best
   final eval
```
The direction `step=m/(sqrt(v)+eps)` has scale of gradient adaptive ~1. `lr=0.02` update logits ~0.02 per step; 5000 steps total possible change ~100 but adaptive. Good.

```
def make_root(n, mode=0, mix_fraction=0.0, rng=None):
    d=np.empty(n); d[0]=1.
    if n>1: i=np.arange(1,n); ratios=(2*i-1)/(2*i); d[1:]=np.cumprod(ratios)
    if mode==1: d=d[::-1].copy()
    if mode==2: d = mix_fraction*d + (1-mix_fraction)*d[::-1] # need define root base first
    d=np.maximum(d,1e-300); S=d.sum(); return d/S
```
For mode2, compute base d then mix.

`init_random_smooth`:
```
def init_random_smooth(n, rng):
  p=np.ones(n)
  x=np.arange(n)/n
  for f in range(1,8):
    ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
    amp=rng.uniform(0.02,0.25)/np.sqrt(f+1)
    p += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x + ph)
  p=np.maximum(p,0.05); p/=p.sum(); return p
```
Could use 10 freqs. Good.

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(old, n):
 old=np.asarray(old,float).ravel(); if old.size==0 or old.sum()<=0: uniform; if size==n return normalized; x_old=np.linspace(0,1,old.size); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n); y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,old); y=np.maximum(y,1e-300); y/=y.sum(); return y
```

`softmax_z` stable:
```
def softmax_z(z):
 z=np.asarray(z,float)
 if z.size==0: return z
 z=z-np.max(z)
 e=np.exp(z)
 s=e.sum()
 if s<=0 or not finite: return np.full_like(z,1/z.size)
 return e/s
```

Optimization of h direct: For each peak k, `seg = p[k-je: k-js+1][::-1]`; add to h[js:je+1]. Ensure slice non-empty.
- If kpeaks=0? no.
- `L = float(np.mean(c[peaks]))`. If peaks all? okay.
- `g = 4.0 * n * p * (h - L)`. Since p sum1, L ~c scale ~1/n, h ~p scale; difference dimension? h has units p; L has units p^2? Wait h_i = Σ q_k p_{k-i} has units p (since q prob). L = smoothed max c has units p^2. Cannot subtract! Mistake. Let's re-evaluate derivative.

For LSE c: L (smoothed max coefficient) has units p^2. h_i = Σ q_k 2 p_{k-i} has units p. The gradient dF/dz = p_i * (dF/dp_i)? dF/dp_i = 4n h_i? For F=2n L, dL/dp_i=2h_i (units p). F=2n L, dF/dp =4n h (units p). Then dF/dz = p_i(4n h_i - p_i*4n? no constrained? For softmax with F=2n L and p sum1 automatically, dF/dz_i = p_i dL/dp_i - p_i Σ p_j dL/dp_j =4n p_i h_i -4n L p_i? Wait dL/dp_i=2h_i (units p). Σ p_j dL/dp_j =2Σ p_j h_j =2L (since q c sum =L). Thus gradient z = p_i*(2h_i) - p_i*(2L) =2 p_i h_i -2 L p_i. Then for F=2nL: 4n p_i h_i -4n L p_i. Here h_i units p, L units p^2; but p_i h_i units p^2, L p_i units p^2. We had g=4n p(h-L). That subtracts L from h before multiplying p, incorrect units! It should be `g = 4.0*n*p*(h - L? no)` Wait p_i*(h_i - ?) only valid if h_i and L have same units. We need `g = 4n * p * h - 4n * L * p` = `4n * p * (h - L?)` only if h and L same units; not. Correct is `g = 4*n*p*h - 4*n*L*p = 4*n*p*(h) - 4*n*L*p`. We can factor p: `4*n*p*(h - L/p?)` not. So previous L-BFGS gradient formula wrong! Important.

Let's fix all gradient formulas.

For LSE objective F(p)=2n L / S^2, p normalized S=1. Derived: dF/dp_i = 4n h_i - 4n L. Wait dimensions: h_i units p, L units p^2?
```
def conv_square_p(p, m):
    n=len(p); M=2*n-1
    A=np.fft.rfft(p, m)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, m)[:M]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c)
    # clip tiny negative
    idx = c < 0
    if np.any(idx): c[idx] = 0.0
    return c
```

```
def polish_lbfgs(p, m_c, m_h, betas, maxfun_per, deadline, label=''):
    n=len(p); p=np.maximum(p,0); S=p.sum(); if S<=... return p, inf
    p=p/S; best_p=p; best_sc=score_p(p,m_c)
    print(...)
    x0=p_to_reduced(best_p,n)
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       # if maxfun_per <=0 continue
       maxfun=int(maxfun_per)
       try:
          res=minimize(lse_reduced_obj, x0, args=(float(beta), n, m_c, m_h), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={...})
          if res.x is not None and finite:
             zfull = reconstruct_reduced(res.x,n)
             p_new=softmax_z(zfull)
             sc=score_p(p_new,m_c)
             if sc<best_sc:
                best_p=p_new; best_sc=sc; x0=p_to_reduced(p_new,n); print
          # else reset
       except Exception as e: print
    return best_p,best_sc
```

Implementation:
```
def peak_adam_z(p, m_c, deadline, max_steps_per=5000, lr=0.02, kpeaks_seq=(3,5,10,20), label=''):
  n=len(p); p=np.maximum(p,0); S=p.sum(); p=p/S
  best_p=p; best_sc=score_p
  z=np.log(p+1e-300); z-=z.mean(); zcap=80; ...
  m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
  done=False
  for kp in kpeaks_seq:
     if time>deadline or done: break
     for st in range(max_steps_per):
       if time > deadline: done=True; break
       p=softmax_z(z)
       c=conv_square_p(p,m_c)
       if kp >= len(c): peaks=np.arange(len(c))
       else: peaks=np.argpartition(c, -kp)[-kp:]
       L=float(np.mean(c[peaks]))
       h=np.zeros(n)
       inv=1.0/len(peaks)
       for kk in peaks:
          k=int(kk); js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(k,n-1)
          if je>=js: h[js:je+1] += inv * p[k-je:k-js+1][::-1]
       g=4.0*n*p*(h-L)
       gm=g.mean(); g-=gm
       if not finite continue
       m=0.9*m+0.1*g; v=0.99*v+0.01*g*g
       delta=m/(np.sqrt(v)+1e-8)
       scale=lr
       maxd=float(np.max(np.abs(delta)))
       if maxd>0:
          if maxd>1.0: scale=1.0/maxd
          z -= scale*delta
       z-=z.mean()
       maxz=float(np.max(np.abs(z)))
       if maxz>80: z*=80.0/maxz
       if st%100==0:
          # p currently =softmax before update? We updated z; need recompute p? Could compute score with z.
          p_new=softmax_z(z); sc=score_p(p_new,m_c)
          if sc<best_sc: best_p=p_new; best_sc=sc; q?
   final eval p=softmax_z(z)
```
The scale condition: `delta` RMS maybe around 1. If maxd > something, we want max update ~lr. Better `step = lr * delta`; if max update > max_update (0.2), scale down. Use:
```
upd=lr*delta
maxupd=float(np.max(np.abs(upd)))
if maxupd>0.2: upd*=0.2/maxupd
z -= upd
```
This keeps each step small. If delta RMS 1, update lr=0.02, max maybe 0.1. Good. `lr=0.05` maybe; use 0.02.

Score every 100; after z update p recompute. Good.

Detailed function code:

```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

```
def make_root(n, mode=0, mix=0.8):
    d=np.empty(n,float)
    if n<=0: return np.array([0.5])
    d[0]=1.0
    if n>1:
        i=np.arange(1,n,dtype=float)
        rat=(2.0*i-1.0)/(2.0*i)
        d[1:]=np.cumprod(rat)
    if mode==1:
        d=d[::-1].copy()
    elif mode==2:
        base=d.copy(); d=mix*base+(1.0-mix)*base[::-1]
    d=np.maximum(d,1e-300)
    s=d.sum()
    return d/s
```
mode values: 0 root,1 reversed,2 mix. If mode2 with mix 0.7 root +0.3 reversed.

```
def init_random_smooth(n, rng):
    p=np.ones(n)
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)/float(n)
    for f in range(1,10):
       ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.20))/float(np.sqrt(f+1.0))
       p += amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*f*x+ph)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.05)
    p /= p.sum()
    return p
```

```
def resample_seq(old,n):
    old=np.asarray(old,dtype=float).ravel()
    old=np.maximum(old,0.0)
    s=old.sum()
    if s <= 0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    old/=s
    if old.size==n: return old.copy()
    xo=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old.size)
    xn=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    y=np.interp(xn,xo,old)
    y=np.maximum(y,1e-300)
    y /= y.sum()
    return y
```

```
def softmax_z(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=float)
    if z.size==0: return z
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
       return np.full(z.size,1.0/z.size)
    z=z-np.max(z)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0.0:
       return np.full(z.size,1.0/z.size)
    return e/s
```

`conv_square_p`:
```
def conv_square_p(p, m):
    n=len(p); M=2*n-1
    A=np.fft.rfft(p, m)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, m)[:M]
    c = np.array(c, dtype=float, copy=True)  # maybe already
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c
```
But `A*A` complex; irfft returns real. If p has large? no.

`score_p` inf if not finite:
```
def score_p(p, m):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float)
    n=len(p)
    if n==0: return np.inf
    S=float(p.sum())
    if S < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S): return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)): return np.inf
    c=conv_square_p(p,m)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*M/(S*S))
```

`conv_q_p`
```
def conv_q_p(q,p,m):
    n=len(p)
    Q=np.fft.rfft(q,m)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],m)
    h=np.fft.irfft(Q*P,m)
    return h[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Assume q length 2n-1. If q sum zero? no.

`lse_reduced_obj`
```
def lse_reduced_obj(x, beta, n, m_c, m_h):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    if x.size != n-1:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
    z=np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    z[:-1]=x
    z[n-1] = -float(np.sum(x))
    p=softmax_z(z)
    c=conv_square_p(p, m_c)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <=0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax): return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
    scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
    scaled=scaled.astype(float)  # maybe
    scaled[scaled < -745.0] = -745.0
    e=np.exp(scaled)
    es=float(e.sum())
    if es <=0.0 or not np.isfinite(es): return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
    q=e/es
    L=cmax + float(np.log(es))/beta
    if not np.isfinite(L): return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
    h=conv_q_p(q,p,m_h)
    obj=2.0*n*L
    if not np.isfinite(obj): obj=1e100
    g=4.0*n*p*(h-L)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
       return obj, np.zeros(n-1)
    gsum=float(np.sum(g))
    if np.isfinite(gsum): g -= gsum/float(n)
    gx = g[:-1] - g[n-1]
    gx = np.asarray(gx,dtype=float)
    gx[~np.isfinite(gx)] = 0.0
    return obj, gx
```
Note `scaled[scaled < -745.0]` modifies array; scaled is new. Good. If beta float large, `np.log(es)` may be -? okay. If beta very small, fine. Beta >0. If beta=0 invalid. Ensure betas >0.

`p_to_reduced`:
```
def p_to_reduced(p,n):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=p.sum(); if s<=...: p=np.full(1,1)
    p=p/s
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
    z-=z.mean()
    return z[:-1].copy()
```

`reconstruct_z`:
```
def reconstruct_z(x,n):
    z=np.empty(n); z[:-1]=x; z[n-1]= -float(np.sum(x)); return z
```

`polish_lbfgs`:
```
def polish_lbfgs(p, m_c, m_h, betas, maxfun, deadline, label=''):
    n=len(p)
    p=np.maximum(np.asarray(p,dtype=float),0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=...: return p, np.inf
    p=p/s
    best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=score_p(p,m_c)
    if label: print(...)
    x0=p_to_reduced(p,n)
    maxfun=int(max(10, maxfun))
    maxit=int(max(10, maxfun))
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       beta=float(beta)
       if beta <=0: continue
       try:
          res = minimize(lse_reduced_obj, x0, args=(beta,n,m_c,m_h), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'presolve':False? no L-BFGS doesn't use, 'maxiter':maxit,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':50,'disp':False})
          if res is not None and res.x is not None:
             z=reconstruct_z(res.x,n)
             p_new=softmax_z(z)
             sc=score_p(p_new,m_c)
             if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_p=p_new.copy(); best_sc=sc; x0=p_to_reduced(p_new,n); print...
       except Exception as e:
          print(...)
    # final maybe if last x improved but not? objective maybe not exact; we already evaluate after each beta. If not improved, no.
    return best_p,best_sc
```
Note `options` for L-BFGS-B does not accept 'presolve'. Omit. It accepts 'disp'. Good.

If beta low, objective smooth; L-BFGS may converge before maxfun, but exact score may not improve at that beta? We evaluate res.x. If exact score not improve, maybe res.x worse; keep x0 best. But maybe res.x same objective but exact different? Not update. If last x not best, next beta starts from best, potentially missing path? Could be okay. def make_beta_schedule(n, num=5):
```
    low=500.0
    hi=2.0e6 * (float(n)/8192.0)
    hi=max(hi, 10000.0)
    hi=min(hi, 50000000.0)
    out=[]
    if num==1: return [int(hi)]
    for i in range(num):
       if i==0: val=low
       else: val=low * (hi/low) ** (float(i)/float(num-1))
       out.append(int(min(100000000.0, max(10.0, val))))
    return out
```

```
def blend_line_search(a,b,m):
    a=np.maximum(a,0); b=np.maximum(b,0)
    sa=a.sum(); sb=b.sum()
    if sa<=... or sb<=...: return a/max? , score
    if sa<=: return b... 
    aN=a/sa; bN=b/sb
    best=aN.copy(); best_sc=score_p(aN,m)
    ts=[0.0,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,1.0]
    best_t=0.0; evals=0
    for t in ts:
       if evals>10: break
       p=(1-t)*aN + t*bN
       sc=score_p(p,m); evals+=1
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_t=t; best=p
    # refine around best_t
    low=max(0.0,best_t-0.1 if best_t>0 else 0.0)
    high=min(1.0,best_t+0.1 if best_t<1 else 1.0)
    for _ in range(5):
       if time.time? no deadline? Could pass max_eval only. Use for 5 grid.
       # simple 5-point between low/high maybe if best_t=0 low 0 high0.1
       if high<=low: break
       for frac in [0.25,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.75]:
           t=low+(high-low)*frac; p=...
    return best,best_sc
```

LP solve:
```
def solve_lp_for_update(p, n, time_limit=20.0):
    # p normalized
    a = p * np.sqrt(2.0*float(n))
    m=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    c=conv_square_p(a,m)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if M <=0 or not finite: return None
    A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=float)
    for k in range(2*n-1):
       js = max(0, k-n+1)
       je = min(k, n-1)
       if je >= js:
          A[k, js:je+1] = a[k-je : k-js+1][::-1]
    cobj = -np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    bub = np.full(2*n-1, M, dtype=float)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    options = {'time_limit': int(max(1.0,time_limit))}
    try:
       res=linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=bub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=options)
    except Exception:
       try:
          res=linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=bub, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
       except Exception:
          return None
    if res is None: return None
    x=res.x
    if x is None: return None
    b=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    b=b[~np.isnan(b)]? if len mismatch.
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    s=b.sum()
    if s<=1e-12 or not finite: return None
    return b/s
```
`time_limit` if int too small? okay. If options has unsupported key, linprog may throw; fallback.

```
def lp_global_search(start_p, n, deadline, label=''):
    p=start_p.copy(); p normalize; m_c,m_h; best_p,best_sc
    # initial polish short
    pol_deadline=[]; if time + 120 < deadline: p,sc=polish_lbfgs(...)
    if sc<... update
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       rem=deadline-time
       if rem < 60: break
       tl=min(25.0, max(10.0, (rem-120.0)/10.0))
       b=solve_lp_for_update(best_p, n, tl)
       if b is None: break
       # maybe line search
       pb, sc = blend_line_search(best_p,b,m_c)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
          best_p=pb; best_sc=sc; no_improve=0; print
       else:
          no_improve +=1; if no_improve>=3: break
    return best_p,best_sc
```

```
def regularize_candidates(p, m):
    p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=: p uniform
    p=p/s; n=len(p)
    best=p.copy(); best_sc=score_p(p,m)
    for eps in (0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02):
       q=(1.0-eps)*p + eps*(1.0/float(n))
       q=np.maximum(q,0); q/=q.sum(); sc=score_p(q,m)
       if sc<best_sc: best=q; best_sc=sc
    return best,best_sc
```

```python
"""
Improved search for non-negative step-function coefficients minimizing
2*n*max(autocorr_square)/(sum^2).

Main components:
- A robust log-domain L-BFGS optimizer for a log-sum-exp smoothed objective.
- A fast peak-directed Adam polish that alternates on a small set of active peaks.
- A small LP-direction search: for the current sequence a, solve
  max sum(b)  s.t.  b>=0,  (a*b)_k <= M,
  then line-search blends of a and b.
- Multiple diverse initializations (analytic (1-z)^(-1/2)-type root, mirrors,
  smooth random, parent resampling), multiple lengths, and final polishing.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def make_root(n, mode=0, mix=0.8):
    """Analytic initializations based on coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2)."""
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([0.5], dtype=float)
    d = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    d[0] = 1.0
    if n > 1:
        i = np.arange(1, n, dtype=float)
        ratios = (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
        d[1:] = np.cumprod(ratios)
    if mode == 1:
        d = d[::-1].copy()
    elif mode == 2:
        base = d.copy()
        d = mix * base + (1.0 - mix) * base[::-1]
    d = np.maximum(d, 1e-300)
    s = float(d.sum())
    return d / s


def init_random_smooth(n, rng):
    """Smooth positive random initialization."""
    p = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
    for f in range(1, 10):
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.22)) / float(np.sqrt(f + 1.0))
        p += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.05)
    p /= p.sum()
    return p


def resample_seq(old, n):
    """Linearly resample an old sequence to length n and normalize."""
    old = np.asarray(old, dtype=float).ravel()
    old = np.maximum(old, 0.0)
    s = float(old.sum())
    if old.size == 0 or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=float)
    old = old / s
    if old.size == n:
        return old.copy()
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old.size)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    y = np.interp(xn, xo, old)
    y = np.maximum(y, 1e-300)
    y /= y.sum()
    return y


def softmax_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    if z.size == 0:
        return z
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / float(z.size), dtype=float)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / float(z.size), dtype=float)
    return e / s


def conv_square_p(p, m):
    """Linear convolution p * p using an FFT length m >= 2*len(p)-1."""
    n = len(p)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)[:M]
    c = c.copy()
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def score_p(p, m):
    """Exact score for a coefficient sequence p."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        return np.inf
    S = float(p.sum())
    if S < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.inf
    c = conv_square_p(p, m)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S))


def conv_q_p(q, p, m):
    """
    h_i = sum_k q_k p_{k-i}, with p length n and q length 2n-1.
    """
    n = len(p)
    Q = np.fft.rfft(q, m)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], m)
    h = np.fft.irfft(Q * P, m)
    return h[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def p_to_reduced(p, n):
    """
    Map p in the positive simplex to logits z with zero sum, then return the
    first n-1 coordinates. The last coordinate is -sum(first n-1).
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=float)
    else:
        p = p / s
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    return z[:-1].copy()


def reconstruct_z(x, n):
    z = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    z[:-1] = x
    z[n - 1] = -float(np.sum(x))
    return z


def lse_reduced_obj(x, beta, n, m_c, m_h):
    """
    LSE-smoothed score in reduced logit coordinates.

    Returns (objective, gradient w.r.t. x of length n-1).
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if x.size != n - 1:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=float)

    z = reconstruct_z(x, n)
    p = softmax_z(z)

    c = conv_square_p(p, m_c)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=float)

    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    scaled = np.maximum(scaled, -745.0)
    e = np.exp(scaled)
    es = float(e.sum())
    if es <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(es):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=float)

    q = e / es
    L = cmax + float(np.log(es)) / beta
    if not np.isfinite(L):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=float)

    h = conv_q_p(q, p, m_h)

    obj = 2.0 * float(n) * L
    if not np.isfinite(obj):
        obj = 1e100

    # dF/dz_i = 4 n p_i (h_i - L)
    g = 4.0 * float(n) * p * (h - L)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        return float(obj), np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=float)

    gsum = float(np.sum(g))
    if np.isfinite(gsum):
        g -= gsum / float(n)

    # Gradient in the reduced coordinates z_{n-1} = -sum(x_0...x_{n-2}).
    gx = g[:-1] - g[n - 1]
    gx = np.asarray(gx, dtype=float)
    gx[~np.isfinite(gx)] = 0.0
    return float(obj), gx


def make_beta_schedule(n, num=5):
    low = max(1000.0, 1.0e5 * (float(n) / 8192.0))
    hi = max(low * 10.0, 2.0e6 * (float(n) / 8192.0))
    hi = min(hi, 5.0e7)
    out = []
    if num <= 1:
        return [int(hi)]
    for i in range(num):
        if i == 0:
            val = low
        else:
            val = low * (hi / low) ** (float(i) / float(num - 1))
        out.append(int(min(1.0e8, max(10.0, val))))
    return out


def polish_lbfgs(p, m_c, m_h, betas, maxfun, deadline, label=""):
    """
    Run L-BFGS on the LSE objective from sequence p for increasing betas.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = len(p)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return p, np.inf
    p = p / s

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = score_p(p, m_c)
    if label:
        print(f"  [{label}] initial score = {best_sc:.8f} (n={n})")

    if n <= 1:
        return best_p, best_sc

    x0 = p_to_reduced(p, n)
    maxfun = int(max(20, maxfun))
    maxiter = int(maxfun)

    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        beta = float(beta)
        if beta <= 0.0:
            continue
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lse_reduced_obj,
                x0,
                args=(beta, n, m_c, m_h),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "ftol": 1e-14,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "maxls": 50,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
            if res is not None and res.x is not None:
                z = reconstruct_z(res.x, n)
                p_try = softmax_z(z)
                sc_try = score_p(p_try, m_c)
                if np.isfinite(sc_try):
                    if sc_try < best_sc:
                        best_p = p_try.copy()
                        best_sc = sc_try
                        x0 = p_to_reduced(p_try, n)
                        if label:
                            print(f"  [{label}] beta={beta:.3g} NEW BEST {best_sc:.8f}")
                    elif sc_try < best_sc * 1.05 + 1e-9:
                        # Continue from a mildly worse smoothed point.
                        x0 = p_to_reduced(p_try, n)
        except Exception as e:
            if label:
                print(f"  [{label}] L-BFGS beta={beta:.3g} exception: {e}")
    return best_p, best_sc


def peak_adam_z(p, m_c, deadline, max_steps_per=4000, lr=0.02,
                kpeaks_seq=(1, 3, 5, 10), label=""):
    """
    Fast exact/semi-exact peak-directed Adam polish in logit space.
    Optimizes the average of the top K convolution peaks and uses exact
    subgradient for K=1.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = len(p)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return p, np.inf
    p = p / s

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = score_p(p, m_c)
    if label:
        print(f"  [{label}] peak-adam initial score = {best_sc:.8f}")

    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    adam_m = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    adam_v = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    done = False

    for kp in kpeaks_seq:
        if done:
            break
        for st in range(int(max_steps_per)):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                done = True
                break

            p = softmax_z(z)
            c = conv_square_p(p, m_c)
            if kp >= c.size:
                peaks = np.arange(c.size)
            else:
                peaks = np.argpartition(c, -int(kp))[-int(kp):]
            L = float(np.mean(c[peaks]))
            if L <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(L):
                continue

            h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            inv = 1.0 / float(len(peaks))
            for kk in peaks:
                k = int(kk)
                js = max(0, k - n + 1)
                je = min(k, n - 1)
                if je >= js:
                    h[js:je + 1] += inv * (p[k - je:k - js + 1][::-1])

            g = 4.0 * float(n) * p * (h - L)
            gm = float(np.mean(g))
            g -= gm
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
                continue

            adam_m = 0.9 * adam_m + 0.1 * g
            adam_v = 0.99 * adam_v + 0.01 * (g * g)
            delta = adam_m / (np.sqrt(adam_v) + 1e-8)
            upd = lr * delta
            maxupd = float(np.max(np.abs(upd)))
            if maxupd > 0.2 and np.isfinite(maxupd):
                upd *= 0.2 / maxupd
            z -= upd
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            maxz = float(np.max(np.abs(z)))
            if maxz > 80.0 and np.isfinite(maxz):
                z *= 80.0 / maxz

            if st % 100 == 0:
                p_try = softmax_z(z)
                sc_try = score_p(p_try, m_c)
                if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_sc:
                    best_p = p_try.copy()
                    best_sc = sc_try
                    if label:
                        print(f"  [{label}] peak-adam K={kp} new best {best_sc:.8f} (step={st})")

    p_try = softmax_z(z)
    sc_try = score_p(p_try, m_c)
    if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_sc:
        best_p = p_try.copy()
        best_sc = sc_try
    return best_p, best_sc


def blend_line_search(a, b, m_c):
    """Line search over convex blends of a and b."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = len(a)

    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    sa = float(a.sum())
    sb = float(b.sum())
    if sa <= 1e-300 or sb <= 1e-300:
        a = a if sa > sb else b
        sa = float(a.sum())
        if sa <= 1e-300:
            a = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=float)
            sa = 1.0
        a = a / sa
        return a, score_p(a, m_c)

    an = a / sa
    bn = b / sb
    best = an.copy()
    best_sc = score_p(an, m_c)
    best_t = 0.0

    grid = (0.0, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0)
    for t in grid:
        p_try = (1.0 - t) * an + t * bn
        p_try = np.maximum(p_try, 0.0)
        p_try /= p_try.sum()
        sc = score_p(p_try, m_c)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = p_try.copy()
            best_t = float(t)

    # Small refinement around best_t.
    if best_t > 0.0:
        lo = max(0.0, best_t - 0.1)
        hi = min(1.0, best_t + 0.1)
    else:
        lo, hi = 0.0, 0.1
    if hi > lo:
        for frac in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
            t = lo + (hi - lo) * frac
            p_try = (1.0 - t) * an + t * bn
            p_try = np.maximum(p_try, 0.0)
            p_try /= p_try.sum()
            sc = score_p(p_try, m_c)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best = p_try.copy()

    best = best / best.sum()
    return best, best_sc


def solve_lp_for_update(p, n, time_limit=25.0):
    """
    For current normalized p, solve
      max 1^T b  s.t.  b>=0, A p b <= M
    where M is current peak. Return normalized b, or None.
    """
    try:
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            return None
        p = p / s

        # Scale to match the state-of-art normalization: sum(a)=sqrt(2n),
        # then M is exactly the objective score.
        a = p * np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
        m_c = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
        c = conv_square_p(a, m_c)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
            return None

        A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=float)
        for k in range(2 * n - 1):
            js = max(0, k - n + 1)
            je = min(k, n - 1)
            if je >= js:
                A[k, js:je + 1] = a[k - je:k - js + 1][::-1]

        cobj = -np.ones(n, dtype=float)
        bub = np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=float)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
        options = {"time_limit": int(max(1.0, time_limit))}

        res = linprog(
            cobj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=bub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options=options,
        )
        if res is None:
            return None
        b = res.x
        if b is None:
            return None
        b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float).ravel()
        if b.size != n:
            return None
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        sb = float(b.sum())
        if sb <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(sb):
            return None
        return b / sb
    except Exception:
        return None


def lp_global_search(start_p, n, deadline, label="LP"):
    """LP-direction search at a fixed moderate length."""
    p = np.asarray(start_p, dtype=float).ravel()
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if p.size != n or s <= 1e-300:
        p = make_root(n, 0)
    else:
        p = p / s

    m_c = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    m_h = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = score_p(p, m_c)
    print(f"  [{label}] n={n} initial score={best_sc:.8f}")

    # First polish with L-BFGS.
    init_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 120.0)
    if time.monotonic() < init_deadline:
        betas = make_beta_schedule(n, 4)
        p_try, sc_try = polish_lbfgs(
            p, m_c, m_h, betas, 2500, init_deadline, label=f"{label}-LBFGS"
        )
        if sc_try < best_sc:
            best_p = p_try
            best_sc = sc_try

    no_improve = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 70.0:
            break
        tl = min(30.0, max(10.0, (rem - 120.0) / 10.0))
        b = solve_lp_for_update(best_p, n, tl)
        if b is None:
            break
        pb, sc = blend_line_search(best_p, b, m_c)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
            best_p = pb
            best_sc = sc
            no_improve = 0
            print(f"  [{label}] LP new best {best_sc:.8f} (n={n})")
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 3:
                break
    return best_p, best_sc


def regularize_candidates(p, m_c):
    """Try small uniform regularization and return the best."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    n = len(p)
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=float)
    else:
        p = p / s

    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = score_p(p, m_c)
    for eps in (0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02):
        q = (1.0 - eps) * p + eps * (1.0 / float(n))
        q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
        q /= q.sum()
        sc = score_p(q, m_c)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = q.copy()
            best_sc = sc
    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    global_deadline = t0 + total_budget - 25.0

    # Load parent if present.
    raw_parent = None
    try:
        raw_parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    except Exception:
        raw_parent = None

    parent = None
    if raw_parent is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(raw_parent, dtype=float).ravel()
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            s = float(arr.sum())
            if arr.size > 0 and s > 1e-12:
                if 512 <= arr.size <= 32768:
                    parent = arr / s
                else:
                    parent = resample_seq(arr, 16384)
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    best_p = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    best_n = 0

    def update_best_local(p, sc):
        nonlocal best_p, best_sc, best_n
        if p is None:
            return
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
        if p.size == 0:
            return
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            return
        p = p / s
        mtmp = next_pow2(2 * p.size - 1)
        sc2 = score_p(p, mtmp)
        if sc is None or not np.isfinite(sc):
            sc = sc2
        else:
            # Use exact recomputed score for consistency.
            sc = sc2
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_sc = sc
            best_n = p.size
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print(f"GLOBAL NEW BEST: {best_sc:.8f} (n={best_n}, t={elapsed:.0f}s)")

    # Parent quick polish.
    if parent is not None:
        n_parent = len(parent)
        m_c_parent = next_pow2(2 * n_parent - 1)
        m_h_parent = next_pow2(3 * n_parent - 2)
        update_best_local(parent, None)
        parent_deadline = min(global_deadline - 1400.0, t0 + 180.0)
        if time.monotonic() < parent_deadline:
            if n_parent <= 4096:
                mf_parent = 3000
            elif n_parent <= 12000:
                mf_parent = 2000
            else:
                mf_parent = 1200
            betas_parent = make_beta_schedule(n_parent, 4)
            p_out, sc_out = polish_lbfgs(
                parent, m_c_parent, m_h_parent, betas_parent,
                mf_parent, parent_deadline, label="PARENT"
            )
            update_best_local(p_out, sc_out)

    # Main diverse optimization at a larger length.
    n_main = 16384
    m_c_main = next_pow2(2 * n_main - 1)
    m_h_main = next_pow2(3 * n_main - 2)

    main_deadline = min(global_deadline - 900.0, t0 + 720.0)
    inits = []
    inits.append(("ROOT", make_root(n_main, 0)))
    inits.append(("REV", make_root(n_main, 1)))
    inits.append(("MIX", make_root(n_main, 2, 0.82)))
    inits.append(("RAND", init_random_smooth(n_main, rng)))
    if parent is not None:
        inits.append(("PARENT-RES", resample_seq(parent, n_main)))

    for lab, p0 in inits:
        if time.monotonic() > main_deadline:
            break
        # Shortened beta schedule for broad exploration.
        betas = make_beta_schedule(n_main, 4)
        if n_main <= 4096:
            mf = 1500
        elif n_main <= 12000:
            mf = 800
        else:
            mf = 450
        sub_deadline = min(main_deadline, time.monotonic() + 130.0)
        p_out, sc_out = polish_lbfgs(
            p0, m_c_main, m_h_main, betas, mf, sub_deadline, label=f"MAIN-{lab}"
        )
        update_best_local(p_out, sc_out)

    # Optional larger-length exploration if time remains and current bound is not close enough.
    if time.monotonic() < global_deadline - 700.0 and (not np.isfinite(best_sc) or best_sc > 1.504):
        n_big = 32768
        m_c_big = next_pow2(2 * n_big - 1)
        m_h_big = next_pow2(3 * n_big - 2)
        big_deadline = min(global_deadline - 650.0, time.monotonic() + 260.0)
        if time.monotonic() < big_deadline:
            p0 = make_root(n_big, 0)
            betas = make_beta_schedule(n_big, 3)
            p_out, sc_out = polish_lbfgs(
                p0, m_c_big, m_h_big, betas, 400, big_deadline, label="BIG-ROOT"
            )
            update_best_local(p_out, sc_out)
            if time.monotonic() < big_deadline:
                p0 = make_root(n_big, 1)
                p_out, sc_out = polish_lbfgs(
                    p0, m_c_big, m_h_big, betas, 350, big_deadline, label="BIG-REV"
                )
                update_best_local(p_out, sc_out)

    # Peak-directed Adam polish on current best.
    if best_p is not None:
        n_best = len(best_p)
        m_c_best = next_pow2(2 * n_best - 1)
        peak_deadline = min(global_deadline - 420.0, time.monotonic() + 280.0)
        if time.monotonic() < peak_deadline:
            if n_best <= 4096:
                steps_per = 8000
            elif n_best <= 12000:
                steps_per = 4500
            else:
                steps_per = 2500
            p_out, sc_out = peak_adam_z(
                best_p, m_c_best, peak_deadline,
                max_steps_per=steps_per, lr=0.02,
                kpeaks_seq=(1, 3, 5, 10), label="PEAK"
            )
            update_best_local(p_out, sc_out)

    # LP-direction search at a moderate length.
    lp_n = 2048
    if best_p is not None and len(best_p) == lp_n:
        lp_start = best_p.copy()
    else:
        lp_start = make_root(lp_n, 0)
        if parent is not None:
            lp_start = resample_seq(parent, lp_n)

    lp_deadline = min(global_deadline - 320.0, time.monotonic() + 360.0)
    if time.monotonic() < lp_deadline:
        p_out, sc_out = lp_global_search(lp_start, lp_n, lp_deadline, label="LP")
        update_best_local(p_out, sc_out)

    # Final L-BFGS polish on global best.
    if best_p is not None:
        n_best = len(best_p)
        m_c_final = next_pow2(2 * n_best - 1)
        m_h_final = next_pow2(3 * n_best - 2)
        final_deadline = min(global_deadline - 35.0, time.monotonic() + 450.0)
        if time.monotonic() < final_deadline:
            if n_best <= 4096:
                mf_final = 9000
            elif n_best <= 12000:
                mf_final = 4500
            else:
                mf_final = 2200
            betas_final = make_beta_schedule(n_best, 5)
            p_out, sc_out = polish_lbfgs(
                best_p, m_c_final, m_h_final, betas_final,
                mf_final, final_deadline, label="FINAL-LBFGS"
            )
            update_best_local(p_out, sc_out)

    # Very short final peak polish.
    if best_p is not None:
        n_best = len(best_p)
        m_c_final2 = next_pow2(2 * n_best - 1)
        last_peak_deadline = global_deadline - 8.0
        if time.monotonic() < last_peak_deadline:
            if n_best <= 4096:
                steps_per = 1500
            elif n_best <= 12000:
                steps_per = 800
            else:
                steps_per = 400
            p_out, sc_out = peak_adam_z(
                best_p, m_c_final2, last_peak_deadline,
                max_steps_per=steps_per, lr=0.01,
                kpeaks_seq=(1, 3), label="FINAL-PEAK"
            )
            update_best_local(p_out, sc_out)

    # Fallback if all failed.
    if best_p is None:
        best_p = make_root(4096, 0)
        best_n = len(best_p)
        best_sc = score_p(best_p, next_pow2(2 * best_n - 1))

    # Final light regularization.
    m_out = next_pow2(2 * len(best_p) - 1)
    best_p, best_sc = regularize_candidates(best_p, m_out)

    # Validate output format.
    out = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=float).ravel()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        out = np.full(64, 0.5, dtype=float)
    else:
        out = out / s

    # Optional verification using the provided evaluator if it is in scope.
    try:
        ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence")
        if callable(ev):
            val = ev([float(x) for x in out])
            print(f"verified evaluate_sequence = {float(val):.8f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    print(f"FINAL internal best = {best_sc:.8f}, length = {len(out)}, elapsed = {elapsed:.0f}s")
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
